							can_construct_building = infrastructure
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									AND = {
										state = 150
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_150 = 1 } }
									}
									AND = {
										state = 151
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_151 = 1 } }
									}
									AND = {
										state = 153
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_153 = 1 } }
									}
									AND = {
										state = 154
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_154 = 1 } }
									}
									AND = {
										state = 156
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_156 = 1 } }
									}
									AND = {
										state = 159
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_159 = 1 } }
									}
									AND = {
										state = 160
										owner = { check_variable = { CLM_locked_160 = 1 } }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						OR = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 150 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_150 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 151 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_151 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 152 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_152 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 153 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_153 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 154 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_154 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 155 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_155 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 156 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_156 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 157 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_157 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 158 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_158 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 159 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_159 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 160 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_160 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 161 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_161 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 162 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_162 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
							AND = {
								check_variable = { CLM_electoral_selected = 163 }
								NOT = { check_variable = { CLM_sen_party_163 = CLM_electoral_party_sel } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
